## kalshi-pull: the job input is a free string (Nov 12, 2026, the owner)

`.github/workflows/kalshi-pull.yml` no longer carries a `choice` list of job names or a `case "$JOB"` allowlist (the old "workflow choice list" and case guard are gone; their history stays in the sections above). `job` is a plain string. The first step requires it to match `^[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]{2,60}$` (bash `[[ =~ ]]`, value passed only through the env var `JOB`, never interpolated into a script), and a step after the checkout of `main` requires `kalshi-scalper/workers/kalshi_pull_jobs/$JOB.json` to exist in that checkout (`test -f`, quoted path, no globbing). Why: so a pull request that only adds a job file never edits the workflow again, and the owner's standing permission below applies cleanly to job-only pull requests. Nothing else changed: dry run default, permissions, concurrency group, timeout, pinned checkout, end date, push step and the 20 runs a day. The checkout is still `ref: main`, so a free string cannot run a job from another branch, and `scripts/kalshi_pull.py` is untouched: it still refuses a job whose status is not active, whose owner is unknown, or whose limits it does not accept. If the job file check fails after a real-run request, the push step's `cd data` fails on an already failed run and pushes nothing. Test KP4.

**Standing permission, as the owner worded it (Nov 12, 2026):** "Main may merge kalshi-pull job files that only read public Kalshi lists and candles, after a security pass, without my typed merge. Runner, workflow, bot, site and recorder changes still need my typed merge N." This workflow change itself is a workflow change and needs the owner's typed merge.

## Shadow logger: one read budget, open interest, calendar, second look (Nov 12, 2026, log only)

`functions/kalshiShadowLib.js` and its caller in `functions/index.js`. **Budget:** every public read a shadow rule needs that the bot does not already make (a series list while the session is off, an open interest candle read, a second look at a row) draws from ONE budget of 3 reads for the whole minute (`makeReadBudget`, `SHADOW_READS_PER_MINUTE`), whatever the number of rules; due series are read oldest-unread first (`planShadowReads`, round robin). Markets the bot already holds are free. The budget only says no, never waits; the shadow step stays the last step of the minute with its time guards. **Schema (optional entry fields, none used by the registered rule):** `oiRiseMin` (the slot's definition: open interest at the candle ending 180 s before the close minus the one ending 360 s before; a missing candle or a failed read means NO row, a rise under the line is no signal; the row carries `oiRiseSeen`), `calendar` (a list of ISO close times, like SLOT_CALENDARS: only a market closing at one is a signal) and `secondRead: true`. **Second look:** `SECOND_LOOK_MS` = 4000: about 4 seconds after the row is written the market is read again and the row gets `askAfter` and `wouldFillAfter` (ask still at or under the rule's `priceMax`); the existing `wouldFill` is unchanged; a row with no second read (budget empty, error, slow) keeps both null, never guessed. No order, key or sizing code is touched; an empty registry is still a complete no-op and the registered GOLDOFF behaves as before. Tests AB1 to AB6 (`test/shadowLoggerABGates.test.js`); GO7 was edited on purpose for the planner name and block length. Needs `bash functions/deploy-sizing.sh`.

## Wheel book recorder (Nov 12, 2026, the owner approved: "Yes, build it"; research only, nothing trades)

`.github/workflows/record-wheelbook.yml` and `scripts/record_wheelbook.py` (spec: `kalshi-scalper/workers/wheelbook/WHEEL_BOOK_RECORDER_SPEC.md` on `claude/idea-desk-b`) record the order books of the sports and esports markets the wheel's cards read, so a card that decides on minute candles can be fill-checked on a real book. **Series (fixed in code):** `KXCS2GAME`, `KXCS2MAP`, `KXCS2TOTALMAPS`, `KXDOTA2GAME`, `KXDOTA2MAP`, `KXDOTA2TOTALMAPS`, `KXESOCCERGAME`, `KXLIGAPROMATCH`, `KXEBASKETBALLGAME`; the four tennis series (phase 2) are in the code behind `PHASE2_ENABLED = False`. **Tiers, from fields known in advance (the scheduled start S in the ticker, read as US Eastern; the market's own open; whether it is still open), never from a result:** SLOW one read a minute, FAST one every 3 seconds; the table is `tier_for`. **Kept:** a book row (best 5 YES and 5 NO bids with sizes) only when that ticker's top 5 changed; a poll row for EVERY poll (poll id, send and receive time, Date header, tier, tickers asked, tickers answered, HTTP status); results (list reads of closed and settled markets every 10 minutes per series) and one-minute candles (one batched read per group of markets the recorder saw close, for that market's recorded window only) as raw answer bodies, gzipped, with a `manifest.json` (the kalshi-pull folder shape); the public trade tape of each market once about 60 seconds after its close (cursor followed to the end, trade ids de-duplicated and NOT stored; the saved pages carry ticker, time, YES price, count and taker side); a coverage row per ticker (polls, 429s, fallback, largest gap, rows stored, tape pages, trades, duplicates dropped, summed size beside Kalshi's volume and the result) and an hourly clock check. Public host `api.elections.kalshi.com` only, no key, no auth header, GET only, redirects refused, a generic User-Agent, two streams (0.5 s and 1.5 s between calls), a 429 waits 2 s doubling to 30, a hard budget of 14,000 reads a run, batches of at most 90 tickers (the batch cap is not confirmed in this repo; the kalshi-pull runner allows 1 to 100). **Branch:** its own weekly orphan branch `wheelbook-<ISO year>w<week>` (a job started late on a Sunday writes to that week's branch even if it runs into Monday), files under `wheelbook/<date>/<HHMM>/`, never main, `quote-data`, `nc-data`, `sports-data` or `kalshi-pull-data`; the built-in token pushes with the six-try loop. Triggered every 15 minutes (`5,35` and `20,50`), each job records up to the next 4 hour boundary, one never-cancelling concurrency group, pinned actions, `contents: write` only. **It stops by itself on 2027-01-31 23:59 UTC** (the workflow's first step and the script); delete the workflow to stop sooner. **Standing rule (h):** these files carry results by design (the result field and prices running to 0 or 1), so they are a FORWARD record from the merge day, not any card's development or held-out sample; the engines read them in a replay mode switched on by hand, tune nothing on them, and a rule registered after recorder data exists takes later dates as its held-out. **Estimates (the spec's numbers, not measured):** about 27 reads a minute on average (spec; the script's own bound is at most 40 on average and 100 at the peak), about 53 FAST tickers at once (95th percentile 76), 30 to 60 MB a day gzipped (2.5 to 5 GB to the stop date across weekly branches), about 2,100 markets a day for the tape. If the first day is over 80 MB, the SLOW tier drops to the best level only (not built). **Runner minutes:** continuous like R6 and NC15, about 43,000 billed minutes a month, free on a public repo and far over the Charter's 250 minute guideline; the owner approved the build and the merge accepts that figure. **Unverified:** the map markets (`KXCS2MAP`, `KXDOTA2MAP`) are all recorded SLOW (the spec says maps 1 and 2 only; the ticker's map number format is not known here, so every map market is taken); nothing was run against the live API in this build (the sandbox's address was rate limited). Tests WB1 to WB13 in `test/wheelBookRecorderGates.test.js`. **Also in this change:** R6 (`record-15m-book`) now records Bitcoin and Ethereum (and gold with them) to 2026-12-31 23:59 UTC (`END_UTC` and `ETH_END_UTC`, the workflow's first step, gates FB10 and FB13 updated on purpose); Ethereum no longer stops on Nov 20. Goes live on merge.
## Second slot: loss caps counted per strategy, higher fractions (Nov 12, 2026, the owner: "Raise limits to the max", "Yes, per-seat caps")

`functions/kalshiSlotLib.js`: the slot's daily and lifetime loss caps are now counted from each strategy's OWN orders (`own` in `slotOrderCheckWith`), not from every slot order together, so one seat's losses can never use up another seat's room and each seat's own kill binds first. The base fractions are raised to per order 2.5%, daily 2.5% and lifetime 7.5% (window 2% and open total 3% unchanged) so the dollar ceilings ($20, $20, $60) are reachable at a base of a few hundred dollars and up; the ceilings themselves are unchanged. Open dollars, window spend and open contracts per market stay slot-wide. **What this changes:** the slot no longer has one lifetime or daily loss total across all its strategies; the totals that bound everything together are the shared 12% account stop and the 5.5% window cap. A slot order whose slotId is missing or not in the registry (a retired id) counts against every seat's daily and lifetime figures (fail closed). OICORE's daily room now ignores EVENT830's losses and the other way round. The owner accepts that several full seat kills plus a bad day elsewhere could trip the shared stop. 10 contract orders ($10) still wait for a bigger base. Tests: `kalshiSlotGates` (the per strategy gate, and caps, H1 and scale gates updated on purpose), `kalshiSlotRunGates`. Needs `bash functions/deploy-sizing.sh` (and `deploy-push.sh` first if the exit watch should carry the same library).

## Strategies page lanes are automatic; gold card never sticks on CHECKING (Nov 12, 2026, the owner)

Page only. **CHECKING fix.** After the accuracy fix the owner's phone showed gold's card on CHECKING for good. The latch read (`readKalshiGoldKill`, kept 10 minutes) could never settle on a phone that was offline or not yet signed in, nothing redrew the card, and a failure kept the card in a state with no way out for 10 minutes. Now `kalshiGoldCardState(cache, now, timeoutMs)` (pure) allows loading only for `KAL_GOLD_READ_TIMEOUT` (6 seconds) after the page first wanted the answer; after that, or on any failed or thrown read, the card says STATE UNKNOWN ("kill state not readable"), a late answer still wins, and a failed read may be retried after a minute instead of ten. One one-shot `setTimeout` (`kalshiGoldDeadline`, armed only while loading) redraws the page at the deadline. **Lanes.** `kalshiStratLaneOf(facts)` (pure) places every strategy from live state: retired only by a registry verdict; off the moment its kill latch trips; probation for a second-slot seat (`KAL_SLOT_REGISTERED` or registry `code.slot`) or a registry Probe whose code is live; live for a running non-seat strategy (gold); on deck for shadow, idea, paper and anything not built. The Live lane shows Live cards, then a Probation group, then an "Off / cooling down" group (a killed gold, with its GOLDOFF return progress); the lane count and header count only strategies that trade. OICORE and EVENT830 therefore sit under Probation. A kill, a return or a new seat needs no page change. Tests AL1 to AL3 (`test/strategiesPageGates.test.js`); GK6 and GK7 were updated on purpose for the grouping and the pure state function. Live on merge.

## Strategies page accuracy fix (Nov 12, 2026, the owner)

Page only (plus `strategies-registry.json`); no trading code. **(1) Gold's kill state is read, not assumed.** The gold card showed HEALTHY and a tripwire counter after gold's kill latch had tripped. The page now makes ONE plain admin read of `kalshiLiveControl/sizing` (`readKalshiGoldKill`, returns only the `goldKill` field; `kalshiGoldEnsure`, cached ten minutes like the shadow rows, no timer, no write) and `kalshiGoldKillState` mirrors the bot's `goldKillActive`: killed only for a tripped latch of this generation, armed only when the checker recorded this generation as not tripped, and EVERYTHING else (a failed read, a missing field, which the tick's full write of the sizing document can cause until the next replay, an old generation) is unknown. Killed shows a KILLED badge, a gauge reading OFF and, on the card itself, "Gold is off: the kill rule tripped; it returns only by the written return rule (...)" with the GOLDOFF return record (trades of 60, losses of 10, day of 7, cash PF) from the shadow rows the page already reads. Unknown or still loading never shows HEALTHY (STATE UNKNOWN or CHECKING, "Kill state not readable"). `test/blankSlateGates.test.js` B2 lists the new method on purpose. **Why the counter read 43:** its window began at the old tripwire start (Nov 9 16:22 UTC) while the latch counts from `GOLD_KILL.rearmAt` (2026-11-10 00:00 UTC); the window now starts there and a test pins the two together. **(2) and (3) Registry:** the approval NO side is Retired (failed the bar, closed Nov 12); CFRTI-K is a Probe row with no live code, drawn as APPROVED, BUILDING, still labelled UNPROVEN (RG2 now allows the label on a Probe row that has no `code.live`). **(4) One count definition:** Live and Probe count what is RUNNING with real money, by the registry stage; the Live lane's count is exactly Live plus Probe; a killed card is counted under Off (the lane note reads "real money, 1 off"); a registry Live or Probe row with no live code is Building. Off and Building cells appear only above zero. With gold off, the header reads Probe 2, Live 0, Off 1. **OICORE's NEAR KILL badge** (drawdown used of the $50 stop at 80% or more) matches the bot's drawdown stop at the bottom step (`min(5 x contracts, 12% of base, 150)`), but the kill that binds first is the net floor (net under -$6 after 40 fills) and the 24 loss streak, which the badge does not watch; and the $50 is only right at 10 contracts with a base of about $417 or more. Reported, not changed. Tests GK1 to GK8 in `test/strategiesPageGates.test.js`. Live on merge; nothing to deploy. **(4) OICORE's badge shows room left against the kill that binds first.** The Strategies page no longer shows OICORE's fixed $50 drawdown figure or gauge; the badge and gauge now use the closer of the loss streak (of 24) and the net floor (room above the -$6 line, counted from the same settled cash rows since its review date, active from 40 fills), with `NEAR KILL` when 80% of the closer kill is used (`kalshiBoardKill`, `kalshiStratGauge`; gates OK1 and the edited SB5, SP, registry gates). ## Politics recorder self-chains (Nov 12, 2026)

GitHub dropped most scheduled starts of `record-politics.yml` (3 pushed runs since Nov 11 against 48 a day intended), so the workflow now chains itself: after the poll and the data push, a last step (`if: always()`, so a failed poll still chains) sleeps to the next :00 or :30 UTC at least 20 minutes after the run started, then re-dispatches the same workflow on main with the built-in token (`gh workflow run record-politics.yml --ref main`; a dispatch by `GITHUB_TOKEN` does create a run). It needs `actions: write` beside `contents: write`, nothing else. It never chains past 2027-02-28 23:59 UTC (checked before and after the sleep), and a run skipped by the freshness step or past the end date does not chain (another chain or a cron start exists). The cron `7,22,37,52` stays as the restart path if the chain dies; the concurrency group is unchanged (a dispatch while the current run finishes queues one pending run). `timeout-minutes` is 60. **Runner minutes:** about 48 runs a day of up to ~50 billed minutes each, ~2,000 a day (one runner busy all day); free on a public repo, but far over the Charter's 250 a month guideline, flagged for the owner. Request rate is unchanged (the freshness skip keeps it near one poll per 30 minutes). Gates PR3, PR4 and PR10 updated on purpose; new gate PR11.

## CFRTI-K, a Trial seat in the second slot (Nov 12, 2026, the owner approved a capped Trial seat; below the research bar)

`SLOT_STRATEGIES` adds `CFRTIK` (`functions/kalshiSlotLib.js`; pre-registration `kalshi-scalper/README.md#SLOT-CFRTIK`; spec and parity vectors from Idea Desk A's `claude/idea-desk-a`): Bitcoin 15 minute only, ONE look at 120 seconds before the close, the cheaper side when its ask is 0.05 to 0.30, the YES spread is 3c or less and a model on the Bitcoin perpetual (`KXBTCPERP`) one minute candles says the side is worth at least 5c more than its ask; 3 contracts, an immediate-or-cancel order at the ask seen (no slip), held to settlement (`SLOT_HOLD_ALLOWED` is OICORE, EVENT830, CFRTIK). Kill rules in code: net below -$18 after 5 settled fills, or 30 losses in a row; never re-armed automatically. `reviewedAt` 2026-11-12 (`SLOT_LAST_REVIEWED_ON` moved to match); forward read 2026-11-20. **Development evidence is thin** (cash profit factor about 1.09 over 610 trades, z 0.71 against a bar of 5.46; fee rounding at small size is about the size of the mean): this is a probe, not a finding, and its fills are never research evidence.

**The decision is a pure function** (`functions/kalshiCfrtiKLib.js`: no I/O, no clock, no randomness; `test/cfrtiKParityGates.test.js` PV1 pins it to the desk's 20 vectors, same decision and reason, numbers within 1e-9). Any missing input is no order: the quote, the strike, the opening mark (close minus 900 s), the decision minute's mark (close minus 120 s), or fewer than 6 usable log changes. A candle is keyed by the end of its minute and never replaced by an older one.

**The wait.** The perp candle that ends at close minus 120 s is published a little after that instant, and the minute function starts a few seconds after it, so the engine (`kalshiSlotRunLib.js`, `readPerpCandles` and `sleep` injected by `index.js`; the ticker `KXBTCPERP` is fixed in `index.js`, keyless GET through `liveRequest` with a 3 s abort and no retry) polls for that candle every 1.5 s, for at most 10 s from when the seat starts and never past 10 s into the minute (the stale gate is 20 s), then sends nothing (`cfrti_no_perp_candle`). It reads the candles BEFORE the market, so the ask it judges is as fresh as the candle. **It is evaluated last**: the jobs are sorted so a seat that waits runs after every other strategy's order of that minute is sent, so the wait cannot hold up another order (at the minute this seat can fire, OICORE and EVENT830, whose windows end at 120 s, have normally already fired in the previous tick). What it still does: the minute function's later steps (settle sweep, account snapshot, exit alarm, shadow tick) are delayed by up to about 10 s, once per quarter hour, only when the candle is late. The entry window is 125 to 75 s left; `SLOT_ENTRY_LEFT_MIN_SEC_REF` (60) lets ONLY a strategy with `entry.refModel` go below the global 120 s floor.

**Logged on every evaluation (log only, nothing reads it back):** `perpAgeMs` (age of the newest perp candle at the decision), `perpPresent`, `perpWaitMs`, `perpTries`, merged into every skip record's `detail` of that market and written on the order with `refModel`, `refEdge`, `refChance`, `refAsk`. Skip codes `cfrti_*` per refusal (the report's skip section lists them unchanged). **Sharing Bitcoin with OICORE:** the same side stacks only inside the slot's open cap of 10 contracts per market, so a window where OICORE already holds 10 gives CFRTIK no room (a `cap` skip); the opposite side is a hedge and is refused by the direction lock. Tests CK1 to CK10 (`test/kalshiSlotRunGates.test.js`), PV1 to PV7. Gates that pinned two strategies or `OICORE, EVENT830` were updated on purpose, as was the engine's require list (one more library) and `deploy-latest.sh`, which now sends a registry change to the exit watch too. Page: `KAL_SLOT_REGISTERED`, `KAL_STRATEGY_INFO`, the Rules sentence, the Start confirmation, the Strategies page card, the Own stops line, `strategies-registry.json` (the old `CFRTI-K` Idea row became this Probe row), the changelog. The off-equivalence fixtures are untouched. **Deploy IN THIS ORDER:** `bash functions/deploy-push.sh` FIRST (an older exit watch does not know the id and would give an open CFRTIK order the 25c fallback stop, which sells a 5c to 30c longshot at once), then `bash functions/deploy-sizing.sh`. **Not verified:** the perp candle route's answer shape and its publication delay (the bid and ask `close` fields are taken from `scripts/record_perps_candles.py`; the delay is exactly what the logged `perpAgeMs` will measure), and whether the route accepts an `end_ts` in the future.

**Two verified caveats (Nov 13, 2026, research facts, no live figures).** (1) With the rows that ran at least 1 cent by the next minute dropped, the registered edge reads far below 1.0. That is a diagnostic, partly driven by selection, but it says the edge sits in rows a late order cannot get. (2) An immediate-or-cancel buy at the ask seen filled about two thirds of the time at 2 to 10 seconds, while the engine waits up to 10 seconds for the perp candle, so a fill at the ask seen is not the base case.

## Wheel book recorder fix: closed markets, per-market polls, 48 hour results (Nov 12, 2026, from the History Miner's read of the first real data)

Research only, public Kalshi reads, nothing trades. The first data (branch `wheelbook-2026w46`) showed one fault and two gaps in `scripts/record_wheelbook.py`. **Fault:** a market counted as closed only when it had left the open list AND the clock was past its listed `close_time` minus 5 s, but the close time on the open listing is the far expiry (about 14 days for e-soccer and e-basketball, 37 to 46 hours for CS2, Dota and Liga Pro). So settled markets were polled every 3 s to that expiry (335 of 504 markets were already finalized and had about 4,066 polls each), dead books filled the batches, and the tape and candle reads (which start only after a market is moved to closed) never ran. **Fix:** a market that is no longer in the open list is closed at once (a listing that did not complete changes nothing). `market_closed_at` then reads the market once: its own `close_time` if the status is not open and the time is already past, else the discovery round that saw it gone. That time replaces the listed one (the coverage row's `close_time` and a note keep the listed value), and the tape (once, about 60 s later, never earlier than now) and the one minute candles are read to it. A market is still polled until the next discovery round (every 5 minutes) notices it gone, so about 100 polls at most, not thousands. **Gap 1, the second look:** `polls.csv` said how many tickers a poll asked for, not which. New file `polls_tickers.csv` (`poll_id`, `tier`, `chunk`, `tickers` joined by `|`) is written BEFORE the read, and only when the asked set of that tier and chunk changes; a poll with no row of its own was asked for the set in the latest earlier row of the same tier and chunk. Chosen over a book row on every poll because the asked set changes only a few hundred times a day (tens of KB), where a row per poll per market in the last 10 minutes is tens of thousands of 26 column rows. **Gap 2, results:** besides the 2 hour read every 10 minutes, a 48 hour read of closed and settled markets runs when each job starts and then every 6 hours, so a gap between jobs does not lose a result (about 18 extra list reads, a few pages each, per 6 hours; the budget of 14,000 reads is unchanged). It re-reads whatever is closed or settled in the last 48 hours, not market by market until final. **Documented, not changed:** 62 markets were first seen after their fast window began (`seen_late` says so), and 14 CS2 markets have a blank `open_time` (recorded anyway, slow from first sight). **Estimated size change (not measured):** book rows fall sharply (the dead books stop: at most 100 polls per closed market instead of about 4,066) and the batches shrink from 90 tickers to the real open set, so the book and poll files should drop by well over half; the new tape and candle answers (about 2,100 markets a day, mostly small) add back some tens of MB a day; the new `polls_tickers` file adds tens of KB. Overall the day may land about the spec's 30 to 60 MB or below; read the first day. **Not verified:** nothing was run against the live API; the market read's `status` and `close_time` fields are taken from the open listing's shape and the finalized sample, and an unreadable one falls back safely to the discovery round. Unchanged: public host only, no key, GET only, redirects refused, weekly orphan branch, six try push, never cancelling concurrency, stop date 2027-01-31, request budget and pacing. Tests WB14 to WB17 in `test/wheelBookRecorderGates.test.js`.
## One shared strategy state (Nov 12, 2026, the owner: "the bot status stuff isnt updated to reflect all strategies, and it should communicate with the other pages because itll display irrelevant information as we rotate")

`kalshiStrategyState(facts)` (`index.html`, pure) is the ONE place the page decides whether a strategy is trading: it returns id to `armed`, `off` (kill latch tripped, cooling down), `shadow`, `retired` or `unknown`. `kalshiLiveStateNow(now)` feeds it from what the page already reads (gold's kill latch through `kalshiGoldCardState`, the registry file, `KAL_SLOT_REGISTERED`, the shadow rules); a registry `Retired` verdict wins, a row with `code.live` and stage Live or Probe is armed, gold is `unknown` while the latch is loading or unreadable. Consumers: the Bot status next look ring (`kalshiNextEntry(now, states)`: each `KAL_LOOKS` entry carries `sid` and `who`, only armed ones count, an off one is named under the ring as "off, cooling down", no armed look shows "No strategy armed"), the Needs a look alert (`kalshiSilentMarkets(..., states)`: a market whose strategy is not armed is never called silent), the Strategies table status ("Off, cooling down") and the Strategies page lanes (`liveState[s.id] === 'off'`). The Bot page now starts the latch and registry reads too (`kalshiGoldEnsure`, `kalshiRegistryEnsure`) and both pages redraw when either answers (`kalshiStateChanged`); no new request type. CFRTIK got its own look (2 minutes before the close). With `states` omitted the two functions behave as before, so older gates are unchanged. Not changed: the notification center (the bot's own `gold kill` event already raises its critical item). A new strategy needs a `KAL_LOOKS` entry with its `sid`; the registry row and the bot's list do the rest. Tests SS1 to SS6 in `test/sharedStrategyStateGates.test.js`; SF9, SC*, S7 and BS1 pass unchanged. Page only, live on merge.

## Combo and wheelbook recorders self-chain (Nov 12, 2026, Desk B's diagnosis)

GitHub dropped most scheduled starts: `record-combo.yml` had 1 scheduled start in about 21 slots (the `combo-data` branch held 2 snapshots, and S3's paper test needs its snapshots), and `record-wheelbook.yml` can lose its starts the same way and leave hours unrecorded. Both now chain like the politics recorder. Common rules: the first step records the run's own start time (`steps.gate.outputs.start`, never `github.run_started_at`), the last step (`if: "!cancelled() && ..."`) re-dispatches ONLY this workflow on main (`gh workflow run <file> --ref main`, built-in token), the end date (2027-01-31) is checked before and after any sleep, `permissions` is `contents: write` and `actions: write` only, the cron stays as the restart path and the never-cancelling concurrency group is unchanged. **Combo:** a freshness step skips a poll when the newest folder on `combo-data` is under 25 minutes old (a skipped run does not chain), and the chain sleeps to the next :11 or :41 UTC at least 25 minutes after the start, so snapshots stay about every 30 minutes; the timeout is 60 minutes. **Wheelbook:** the chain dispatches at once after the data push (no sleep), so the next job queues behind this one in the group and no gap opens when a cron start is dropped; it does not chain for a run past the end date, shorter than 2 minutes, or that wrote no data file, so it cannot spin. **Runner minutes:** wheelbook already ran continuously, so no change; combo goes from about 310 to 430 billed minutes a day to roughly 1,000 to 1,900 (about 48 chained runs, each 6 to 9 minutes of polling plus a sleep of up to about 30 minutes). Free on a public repo, far over the Charter's 250 a month guideline: flagged for the owner. Tests CB14 (`test/comboBookRecorderGates.test.js`), WB14 (`test/wheelBookRecorderGates.test.js`); CB3, CB4, WB3, WB4 updated on purpose.

## Politics recorder chain fixed: it never started its second link (Nov 12, 2026, Desk B's diagnosis)

The self-chaining politics recorder (PR 850) died at its first link: `github.run_started_at` is not a context value, so the start time was empty, the next run was dispatched only about 6 minutes after the poll, and the freshness step (a poll under 25 minutes old) skipped it, and a skipped run does not chain. Fix: the first step records the run's own start time (`steps.gate.outputs.start`), the chain step waits until at least 26 minutes after it (past the 25 minute freshness line) and then to the next :00 or :30. Everything else unchanged (cron restart path, `actions: write`, end date). Gate PR11 updated. Workflow change; needs the owner's typed merge; once merged, a hand dispatch at exactly :00 or :30 starts a chain that holds, and the cron covers restarts.

The same pull request adds six mention series to `kalshi-scalper/workers/POLREC_SERIES.json` (now 51 of at most 60): `KXTRUMPMENTION`, `KXTRUMPMENTIONB`, `KXTRUMPSAY`, `KXWORLDNEWSMENTION`, `KXTHEWEEKNIGHTMENTION`, `KXLASTWORDMENTION` (Desk B's request: mentions are the only politics family with enough events a week, about 6, for a 60 signal block, and nothing recorded them; a series that no longer lists simply returns no markets). Cost: about 6 more reads a poll (about 30 more seconds at the 5 second gap). Gate PR6 now expects 51.

## Strategies page stage board (Nov 12, 2026, the owner: "build it into the live page")

The approved mockup replaces the lane board. `kalshiBoardHtml` draws a loop of four boxes (`KAL_STAGES`: Shadow, Probation, Live, Off / Retired) with a count, the rule for leaving each box and flowing dots along the arrows; tapping a box opens it in place (`kalshiStratStage` redraws from the kept state and glides the boxes with a FLIP slide; a phone shows a 2 by 2 loop, then a four pill stepper), and its panel lists compact rows (name, stage, cash PF, one status word, a thin bar). A row opens in place to its detail (ring with n, rule, today and 7 day net through `kalshiMoney`, kill rule with the closest limit hatched, return or promotion rule with progress) and More holds the sparkline, sizing, thresholds, record, read date and the older detail. Lanes, counts and status words come from the same card state as before (`kalshiStratLaneOf`, gold's kill state), so a killed strategy is in Off / Retired with its return progress (`kalshiGoldReturnStats`) and never in Live; an unknown or loading kill state reads STATE UNKNOWN or CHECKING and says "Kill state not readable", never healthy. The strategy nearest its kill gets a pulse dot. A "Promoted N days ago" tag shows only if a registry row carries a real `promotedOn` date (none does today). The summary bar, Recent activity, the Retired list grouped by cause (inside Off / Retired), Paper and Ideas (a quiet fold in Shadow) and the Details popup (Retired and Paper and Ideas rows) stay. Removed as dead: the lane board, tiles, counts row, `kalshiStratLifecycle`, `kalshiStratTwinFirst`, `kalshiBoardCols`, `kalshiRegistryCardHtml`, `kalshiStratCounts`. Animation: the count-up exception now also covers the whole-number box counts (`data-count`, inside the STRAT-ANIM fence); everything else is CSS and stops under `prefers-reduced-motion` and `data-reduce-motion`. Gates: `test/strategiesStageBoardGates.test.js` ST1 to ST10; edited on purpose: strategiesPageGates (SP4, SP5, SP7, SP12b, SP14, FB1, FB2b, FB4, FB6, GK6, GK7, AL3, RT1), strategyBoardGates (SB11, SB12, SB13), strategyRegistryGates (RG5, RG6), strategiesAnimationGates (SA5, SA13, harness).

## Registry sync: wheel places and closures on the site (Nov 13, 2026, the owner: "these are updated on our site?")

`strategies-registry.json` had one Shadow row and none of the Head of R&D's wheel places or the closures of Nov 12 and 13. Now it carries them: **19 Shadow rows** (`WH-` ids: CFRTI-K on Bitcoin admitted but mute until the perps archive carries its ticker, five more CFRTI-K places waiting for it, seven non-crypto depth-cell places counting from the auditor's "closed" on the frozen definition sheet, five in-play jump places and the e-soccer tie place with no log source yet) and, as Retired, four withdrawn forex depth-cell places and the closed rules (`CL-` ids: MAPBREAK, REMATCH, TIE3, WXDAYEND, perp against binary, the stock test S2, the politics cost screen, PROPUNDER, PREMOVE, PREDRIFT). **MAPBREAK is closed at both looks on every title, CS2 included** (two builds agree; the Head of R&D confirmed Nov 12, 7:49 PM CT); it is not pending. TIE3 and WXDAYEND are Retired on the owner's EA message and say "to confirm" in notes until their closing read is in the ledger files. No live figure and no research figure is stored.

**Automatic.** `scripts/sync_registry.py` (stdlib, reads only `git show origin/rnd-queue:WHEEL_ADMIT_LIST.md` and `RND_QUEUE.md`) regenerates only rows whose id starts `WH-` or `CL-`; every other row (live strategies, hand notes) is copied through untouched, and the output is idempotent. It exits 1 and writes nothing if a line a row rests on is gone from the ledger (fail closed). `.github/workflows/sync-registry.yml` runs it at minute 17 of hours 1, 7, 13 and 19 UTC and on demand, runs the registry and live-figure gates, and only then opens or updates a pull request from the bot branch `registry-sync/auto` (the one push is `--force-with-lease` to that branch; it never pushes to main and never merges). It needs the repository setting "Allow GitHub Actions to create and approve pull requests". The owner merges each sync PR. To add a place or closure, edit the catalog in the script, which a reviewed pull request does. Page: the Shadow lane lists the 19 rows as rows (Retired stays folded by cause); no page code changed. Tests `test/registrySyncGates.test.js` RS1 to RS4; RG5 was edited on purpose to leave the generated `WH-` rows out of its fixed pipeline list. Page and registry only, live on merge; nothing to deploy.

## Strategies page: one Live box (Nov 13, 2026, the owner's popup via the EA: "Merge into one Live box")

## Strategies page: a strategy row opens a chart sheet (Nov 13, 2026, the owner: "go back to the original idea of it being a pop-up and displaying the current information but with charts involved", option A, "Sheet")

Page only. A tap on a Live, Off or Shadow row no longer opens a dropdown (`#stratBody .rw > .sx` is never shown); it opens one pop-up, `#spChart` (outside `#stratBody`, so the minute's redraw never closes it; `kalshiStratChart.open` and `.refresh`, called at the end of `renderStrategiesPage`, copy the redrawn row back in, so figures stay live and Privacy view re-masks them). It holds the row's head, then for a computed strategy `kalshiStratChartsHtml` (pure): Settled, Win rate, Avg win, Avg loss; a chart with Cash (running cash result over every settled trade, dashed zero), Rolling PF (rolling 30 trade cash PF on 0 to 3, dashed 1.0, dotted 1.3) and Daily (cash by Central day for the last 14 days, `kalshiStratDaily`, losses as outlined bars below the line) switched by three buttons (`data-seg`, kept for the session); a gauge for the closest kill (the same `kalshiStratGauge` the row's bar uses) and one for a trial seat's fills of 90 (progress, never a pass); and the last 60 trades as dots (wins above the line, losses grey below). Then everything the dropdown held (rule, today and 7 day, kill and return lines, More opened). The data is the orders the page already holds (`rows` and `daily` added to each live card in `renderStrategiesPage`); no new read, no timer, no storage, no write. An empty record says so and draws no line. Dots and the end point are zero-length round-capped lines so the stretched chart keeps them round. Centered (880px) on a desktop, a bottom sheet at 560px and under; Close, a tap outside or Escape closes it and focus goes back to the row. The open plays once (a line draw, the gauges fill, the bars rise, `sc-play`), off under both reduce-motion switches; no figure counts up. The top box and the loop are unchanged; the old `rw-opening` fill and its keyframes are gone. Gate ST14 (`test/strategiesStageBoardGates.test.js`); ST7's reduce-motion list now names the sheet's animations.

## Rotation controller, replay-only dry run (Nov 13, 2026, piece D of `docs/ROTATION_LANE.md` v1.3)

`functions/kalshiRotationLib.js` and `scripts/rotation_replay.js`: a pure module that, given shadow rows and rule definitions, REPLAYS the lane's written rules and returns a decision log. **`replay()` exercises sections 4 to 6 plus the seat decision:** block counting (60 fillable signals, or 150 for a favourites rule, on at least 7 Central days with 10 of the rarer outcome, expiry at 21 days, whole cents rounded down, 10W >= 13L and W >= L in each half, NO single-day test since v1.3, a 30 signal confirmation at W >= L, rulings (a) to (u), the NC15 start 2026-11-13T02:40:00Z), the probation or full seat decision (sections 5 and 5a), and the kills and performance exits of section 6 on a seat's own orders; after a kill or exit the log carries a SEAT_ENDED line and the place reads `seatState: ended`. **Sections 7 (`returnDecision`: cooling and return) and 8 (`slotCaps`, `orderAllowed`, `totalAtRisk`: the total at risk) are exported pure functions only, not called by `replay()`.** It honours its stated `nowMs` (rows decided, second looks and results after it do not exist yet; a result needs `settledAtMs`, else `closeMs`, to be final), dedupes one signal per rule, ticker and side (ruling (s)), clamps `secondLookMaxMs` to 4000 or 6000, and fails closed in the live-against-shadow check when a fill or shadow price is missing. **The probation kill defaults to the built behaviour, a net floor of minus $18 (section 4a); `probationKill: "drawdown"` selects the rule text of 5a.4, and the log header (first line, kind HEADER) names which one ran.** The slot caps are copied from `kalshiSlotLib.js` (2.5% an order, 2.5% a day, 7.5% lifetime) and a drift gate (RR_CAPS) compares them in the test only. **Status: replay-only, not wired, not live.** The module requires nothing, reads no clock, has no randomness, places no order, makes no network or Firestore call; the CLI writes only stdout, or a NEW file given with `--out=` inside the repo or the temp folder (an existing file is refused). `test/rotationReplayGates.test.js` RR_ISO proves nothing under `functions/` or in `index.html` references it. Open choices are comment blocks A1 to A17 at the top of the module. Owner decisions to list: where the caps come from, whether probation needs the premise test WHEEL0 (A14: the module says no), and A6 (net floor or drawdown). Code and tests only, nothing to deploy. Tests RR1 to RR22 and RR_CAPS, RR_NOW, RR_DUP, RR_A9, RR_CLAMP, RR_ENDED.
## Wheel seat wiring, built inert (Nov 13, 2026, piece E of `docs/ROTATION_LANE.md`)

`functions/kalshiWheelLib.js` (pure, no I/O) and `docs/WHEEL_SEAT_WIRING.md`: the rails for rotating seats in the second slot, with NO seat. `WHEEL_SEATS` and `WHEEL_RULE_IDS` are frozen empty lists, `WHEEL_FULL_SEATS_ENABLED` is false, and nothing under `functions/`, `index.html` or `firestore.rules` imports or names the library (gate W10), so no deployed function changes: `SLOT_STRATEGIES` is unchanged and `offEquivalence.json` and `offEquivalenceIndex.json` are byte identical. A seat maps to a slot strategy with a fixed one step scale-in and the slot's own validator judges it; the seat validator adds: a rule id from the fixed code-side list, at most 5 probation seats (the whole list is refused on any bad entry), probation exactly 3 contracts, at most $3 an order, the net floor applying from fill 5 at the latest, a seat id that already names a registered strategy or keyed table refused unless allowed at admission, no price at 88c or above, a net floor no looser than minus $18, full 10 contract seats refused until the constant is flipped by a reviewed edit. Caps reuse `slotCaps` with the seat's per order ceiling on top (smaller wins); kill reuses the slot's kill check and latch; the shard check and the live-against-shadow verdict ('wait', 'stay', 'leave seat', not wired to act) are pure functions. Seat orders are ordinary slot orders, so they count in the shared 12 percent stop, the 5.5 percent window cap and the per strategy daily and lifetime caps. Not built: any engine path that can send a seat order, the registry mapping, the replay controller. Admission needs the owner's popup with the exact spec, the Chief of Staff's security review, the shard check and the caps (see the doc). Tests W1 to W10 in `test/wheelSeatGates.test.js`. Nothing to deploy.

## The Wheel view on the Strategies page (Nov 13, 2026, rotation lane piece F)

A compact block under the stage board (`kalshiWheelHtml`, `kalshiWheelStatus`, `kalshiWheelDay`, `#wheel`), headed "Rotating (unproven)" (`docs/ROTATION_LANE.md` section 5.3). The seat boxes are fixed text: no seat is live and each seat needs the owner's popup (`KAL_WHEEL_SEATS`). It draws no profit factor for a wheel place because the page computes none for them. ## Plain words across the site (Nov 13, 2026, the owner: "lets make sure this info is all easily readable by someone who doesn't understand anything", via the EA)

Page and `strategies-registry.json` text only; nothing to deploy and no layout change (the Open interest jump pop-up stays the reference). **Strategies page:** `KAL_BOARD_LIVE`, `KAL_STAGES`, `KAL_BOARD_SHADOW_RULES` and the retired notes are rewritten in everyday sentences (no internal codes or "owner choice" references; numbers kept); the registry's hand rows for L1, OICORE, EVENT830 and CFRTIK copy the same text (so the pop-up and the card agree), with GOLDOFF and S3 reworded. Ratios are written "wins at least 1.3 times what it loses" rather than with a dollar sign, so the Privacy view gates (which forbid a visible "$" figure under the mask) still hold. Labels: the ring says win/loss; the chart sheet says Total made, Average win and loss, Money made over time, Win/loss ratio, last 30 trades (dashed: break even, dotted: target), Nearest stop rule, Stops when and Comes back when; the gauge caption reads "N losses of 24" and "left before its loss limit"; a finished trial says "win/loss x.xx, needs 1.3 to pass". Status words: NEAR KILL is NEAR STOP, KILLED is STOPPED, ARMED is NO TRADES YET, a shadow KILL or RETIRE is DROP. **Glossary:** `KAL_GLOSSARY` and `KAL_GLOSSARY_HTML`, a closed "What the words on this page mean" panel at the bottom of the Strategies page (profit factor, contract, cheaper and favourite side, settlement, stop rule, paper record, Trial, Under review, open interest, longshot, perpetual price model, fill, held-out read, depth). **Bot page Rules:** opens with what a contract is, gives each strategy's plain name beside its code, and explains open interest and the spread; the pinned phrases the freshness and strategy gates read are kept. ## OICORE's limit slip is switched off (Nov 13, 2026, the owner: "Yes, switch it off")

`SLOT_LIMIT_SLIP` in `functions/kalshiSlotLib.js` is empty again (it was `{ OICORE: 0.01 }`), so OICORE's limit is the price it sees; the mechanism (`slotLimitFor`) and the 2c maximum stay for a reviewed edit. Why: the review rule written with the slip on Nov 3 (revert if the orders that filled above the price first seen net below zero) was read on Nov 13 by the refinement chat: the orders that filled 0.95c or more above the first seen ask were net negative while the rest were net positive; part of that is adverse selection a touch-priced limit avoids by not filling, so expect fewer fills. The Bot page Rules text no longer says the limit is 1c above. Needs `bash functions/deploy-sizing.sh` after merge (the owner runs the deploys). Tests LM1 to LM3 (`test/oicoreLimitGates.test.js`) and the slot run gate's limit assertion were changed on purpose.
